Qwen2.5-7B, WikiText-2.\", \"plan_id\": \"plan_005\", \"claim\": [2], \"command\": \"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 python run_budget_sweep.py\", \"server\": \"westd-37397\", \"data_path\": \"/root/ratekv/full_bitwidth_results.json\", \"data_count\": 112, \"model_name\": \"Qwen2.5-7B\", \"seed\": 42, \"checkpoint_path\": \"/root/ratekv/budget_sweep_results.json\", \"log_path\": \"/root/ratekv/budget_sweep.log\"}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"completed_at\": \"2026-05-14T01:13:42.111Z\", \"notes\": \"FP16 PPL=9.81. 2x(budget=0.5): uniform-8bit=10.10, MCKP=10.66 (delta=-0.56, MCKP worse). 4x(budget=0.25): uniform-4bit=15.45, MCKP=11.23 (delta=+4.21, confirmed). 8x(budget=0.125): uniform-2bit=14758.92, MCKP=906.78 (delta=+13852, L0 protection critical).\\n\\n2x归因：PPL profiler在4-bit和8-bit之间分辨率坍缩（per-head PPL delta≈0），solver误判降级免费，将8个head不必要地推至2-bit。uniform 8-bit已近无损（+0.29 vs FP16），per-head优化无收益空间。\\n\\n论文定位：\\n- Claim 2限定为\\\"moderate-to-aggressive compression (≥4x)\\\"\\n- 2x诚实呈现：uniform 8-bit suffices, per-head优化无收益\\n- 8x表述：\\\"even at extreme compression, L0 protection prevents total collapse (906 vs 14759)\\\"，不claim实用性\\n- 整体叙事：\\\"MCKP provides most meaningful improvement at 4x sweet spot\\\"\\n- MCKP优势趋势：2×无优势 → 4× +27% → 8× collapse protection\"}, \"ratequant_greedy_comparison\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"retry_count\": 0, \"created_at\": \"2026-05-14T00:49:20.894Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-14T01:05:19.258Z\", \"spec\": {\"hypothesis\": \"RateQuant 的 greedy Algorithm 1 在离散 {2,4,8,16}-bit action space 上不再 optimal（polymatroid structure 不成立）。在 budget=0.25 下，greedy allocation 的 PPL 应劣于 constrained MCKP (11.23)。\", \"plan_id\": \"plan_008\", \"claim\": [2]}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"checkpoint_path\": \"/root/ratekv/ratequant_greedy_results.json\", \"log_path\": \"/tmp/ratequant_greedy.log\", \"completed_at\": \"2026-05-14T00:59:37.739Z\", \"notes\": \"Greedy PPL=32.45 vs MCKP PPL=11.23, Delta=+21.22。Greedy bit分配: {2:68, 4:30, 8:4, 16:10}; MCKP bit分配: {2:8, 4:100, 8:4}。两者mem_ratio均=0.2500。Greedy将68个非L0 head降至2-bit(MCKP仅8个)，同时保留10个16-bit head。Greedy L0分配: L0H0=8bit, L0H1=8bit, L0H2=16bit, L0H3=8bit——L0实际得到充分保护，greedy的真实问题是非L0的68个head被过度降级到2-bit，compounding error使PPL劣化3倍。非均匀bit间距{2,4,8,16}导致greedy过度激进：16→8省8bit看似高效，驱动大量head继续降至2-bit。结论: RateQuant greedy designed for consecutive integers fails on hardware-aligned discrete bit-widths——RateQuant在自己的连续整数设定{2,3,4,5,6}下works well(Proposition 6 optimality成立)，问题是硬件对齐的非连续{2,4,8,16}打破了polymatroid结构，greedy不再optimal。Paper表述应为'RateQuant greedy fails on hardware-aligned discrete bit-widths'，不应笼统说'RateQuant fails'。Constrained MCKP在practical discrete bit-widths上提供21 PPL gap的巨大优势。\"}, \"compounding_sweep_k10_12\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"retry_count\": 0, \"created_at\": \"2026-05-14T00:49:17.613Z\", \"updated_at\": \"2026-05-14T00:57:05.447Z\", \"spec\": {\"hypothesis\": \"补充 max_2bit={10,12} 数据点，确认 k=8 是 U 型曲线极值点而非 k=8-16 区间内的非极值。\", \"parent\": \"compounding_sweep\", \"plan_id\": \"plan_004\", \"command\": \"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0 python run_compounding_k10_12.py\", \"server\": \"westd-37397\", \"data_path\": \"/root/ratekv/full_bitwidth_results.json\", \"data_count\": 112, \"model_name\": \"Qwen2.5-7B\", \"seed\": 0, \"checkpoint_path\": \"/root/ratekv/compounding_k10_12_results.json\", \"log_path\": \"/tmp/k10_12.log\"}, \"conclusion\": \"success\", \"completed_at\": \"2026-05-14T00:56:46.841Z\", \"notes\": \"k=10: PPL=11.50, bit分配={2:10, 4:98, 8:4}, mem=0.2478; {"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"path\": \"/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_2/projects/ratekv/paper_framing.md\", \"content\": \"# Paper Framing — ratekv\\n\\n## Target Venue\\n- Primary: EMNLP 2026 Main (ARR submission)\\n- Deadline: ARR May 25, 2026 (11 days from today 2026-05-14)\\n- Track: Efficient Methods for NLP\\n\\n## bf16 Consistency Resolution (2026-05-14)\\n\\n**已解决**：qwen_bf16_reeval 确认 bf16 ≈ fp16（baseline delta=0.0006, uniform delta=0.005）。compounding_sweep bf16 rerun 不需要。\\n\\n**eval pipeline 一致性**：exp_e 使用了不同的 eval 参数（baseline=8.82, MCKP=9.57），与主 pipeline（baseline=9.81）不一致。hires_mckp_reeval 用统一 pipeline 重测：MCKP hires bf16=10.9131。**论文使用统一 pipeline 数字**。\\n\\n| 指标 | 统一 pipeline (bf16) | exp_e pipeline (bf16) | fp16 (旧) |\\n|------|---------------------|----------------------|-----------|\\n| Baseline | **9.8106** | 8.819 | 9.81 |\\n| Uniform 4-bit | **15.4454** | — | 15.45 |\\n| MCKP hires (max_2bit=8) | **10.9131** | 9.569 | — |\\n| MCKP lowres (max_2bit=8) | ~11.23 | 11.345 | 11.23 |\\n\\n## Core Insight\\n\\nPer-head KV cache quantization sensitivity varies dramatically across architectures, and the effectiveness of non-uniform mixed-precision allocation is fundamentally determined by the degree of this heterogeneity. We provide both the analysis framework — characterizing when non-uniform quantization helps via per-head sensitivity concentration metrics — and a surprisingly simple method fix: a cardinality constraint (≤k heads at 2-bit) that prevents compounding effects from profiler non-additivity and reduces the search space sufficiently that solver choice becomes irrelevant (greedy and MCKP produce identical allocations).\\n\\n## Core Claims (3 条)\\n\\n1. **Head sensitivity heterogeneity is architecture-dependent and determined by sensitivity *distribution shape*, not GQA ratio or concentration alone**: Per-KV-head quantization sensitivity is extremely non-uniform within models, but whether MCKP can exploit this heterogeneity depends on the *shape* of the sensitivity distribution, not just its magnitude. Critical counterexample: Yi-1.5-9B (GQA 8:1, concentration 0.71 — the *highest* in our lineup) provides *no benefit* from MCKP (7.22 vs 7.22 uniform), while Qwen2.5-7B (GQA 7:1, concentration 0.61) succeeds (+27.3%). The difference is distribution shape: Qwen has a *cluster* of sensitive heads (Layer 0, 4 heads with cross-bitwidth gradients — \\\"spread\\\" pattern, t₁/t₅=0.57), while Yi-1.5 has a single extreme outlier (L4H3, delta=13.12, t₁/t₅=0.90 — \\\"spike\\\" pattern) with sensitivity at only one bitwidth. A joint diagnostic — concentration ≳0.50 AND t₁/t₅ ≲0.70 — correctly separates the beneficial case (Qwen) from all four non-beneficial cases across five architectures. Three shape categories emerge:\\n   - **Spread** (Qwen): high concentration + low t₁/t₅ → multiple heads with cross-bitwidth gradients → MCKP effective\\n   - **Spike** (Yi-1.5, Llama): high t₁/t₅ → single outlier dominates → MCKP ineffective\\n   - **Flat** (Mistral, Gemma-2): low concentration → near-uniform sensitivity → MCKP ineffective (t₁/t₅ irrelevant when concentration < 0.50)\\n\\n2. **Cardinality constraint is the primary intervention; profiling benefit is architecture-dependent**: On non-consecutive discrete bit-widths {2,4,8,16}, unconstrained allocation — whether by greedy (PPL 32.45) or MCKP (PPL 36.92) — produces catastrophic results because per-head independent profiling distortions compound non-additively when many heads are simultaneously assigned extreme quantization. A cardinality constraint (max_2bit≤k) is a simple and sufficient condition: it prevents compounding AND reduces the feasible set to a regime where solver choice is irrelevant — constrained greedy and constrained MCKP produce identical bit allocations and PPL (Δ=0.0000, both 11.2312). The profiling benefit beyond the constraint (hires vs lowres) is architecture-dependent: substantial on high-heterogeneity models (Qwen), marginal on low-heterogeneity ones. The optimal k is determinable via small-scale compounding effect test (plan_004).\\n\\n3. **A joint diagnostic framework correctly separates beneficial from non-beneficial architectures**: Constrained allocation achieves 27.3% PPL improvement on Qwen (spread pattern: concentration 0.61, t₁/t₅=0.57), while providing no benefit on Yi-1.5 (spike: 0.71, t₁/t₅=0.90, ≈0%), Llama (spike: 0.34, t₁/t₅=0.71, +0.2%), Mistral (flat: 0.22, t₁/t₅=0.18, -0.4%), and Gemma-2 (flat: 0.23, t₁/t₅=0.38, -0.3%). The joint condition — concentration ≳0.50 AND t₁/t₅ ≲0.70 — correctly separates the beneficial case from all four non-beneficial cases across five architectures (5/5 accuracy). Concentration alone fails: Yi-1.5 (0.71) exceeds Qwen (0.61) yet gains nothing, because its spike pattern (single outlier, no cross-bitwidth gradient) cannot be exploited. Under aggressive compression (≥5×), constrained allocation prevents catastrophic collapse even on non-beneficial architectures (Llama 5.3×: constrained PPL 57.60 vs uniform 2-bit 2644). This provides practitioners a two-metric diagnostic for when to invest in mixed-precision KV cache quantization.\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n- **Problem**: KV cache quantization typically applies uniform bit-width across all heads. Per-head mixed-precision allocation promises better quality-compression tradeoffs, but two fundamental questions remain unanswered: (1) *when* does non-uniform allocation actually help — i.e., under what architectural and compression conditions does the added complexity pay off? (2) *how* to make existing allocation algorithms (greedy, MCKP) work correctly on hardware-aligned discrete bit-widths {2,4,8,16} where they currently fail catastrophically?\\n\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: RateQuant (May 2026) provides R-D optimal per-head allocation via continuous closed-form (Theorem 2) and discrete greedy (Algorithm 1, optimal on consecutive integers {2,...,6} per Proposition 6). However, on hardware-aligned non-consecutive sets {2,4,8,16}, RateQuant's greedy produces PPL 32.45 (vs optimal ~11.23) — the polymatroid structure that guarantees greedy optimality breaks. Moreover, unconstrained MCKP fares even worse (PPL 36.92) due to compoun\\n\\n... D001\\n- id: D003\\n  date: 2026-05-13 18:12 UTC\\n  summary: MVP 模型从 Llama 3.1-8B 切换到 Qwen2.5-7B，DuoAttention validation 延后\\n  rationale: Llama 3.1-8B 是 HF gated model，用户尚未提供 HF token，GPU 空闲不能等。Qwen2.5-7B 非\\n    gated、GQA 架构、7B 参数量相近，满足 MVP 验证 per-KV-head crossover 假设的需求。代价：DuoAttention post-hoc\\n    validation（D001 rationale 中的 convergent evidence story）暂时无法做——DuoAttention 论文只分析了\\n    Llama 系列。替代方案：(1) 用户后续提供 HF token 后加 Llama 作为第二模型；(2) 在 Qwen 上自行运行 DuoAttention\\n    分类作为替代验证。MVP 核心假设（crossover 存在性 + MCKP 优于 uniform）不依赖 DuoAttention 叙事。注意：Qwen2.5-7B\\n    的 KV head 数（4×28=112）少于 Llama（8×32=256），GQA ratio 更高（7:1 vs 4:1），crossover 比例可能偏低。\\n  related_experiments: []\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D001\\n- id: D004\\n  date: 2026-05-13 21:55 UTC\\n  summary: 1.5B sanity 暴露 attention MSE 与 PPL 脱钩；7B 继续跑但需准备 metric 修正方案\\n  rationale: Qwen2.5-1.5B sanity 结果显示：(1) crossover 只在 8x 极端压缩出现（89.3% heads），此时 PPL\\n    已崩溃（14000+）；(2) 在实用的 4x 压缩下 full MCKP 反而比 quant-only 差 2.77 PPL，甚至比 uniform 4-bit\\n    差 3.41 PPL；(3) 根因是 attention output MSE 系统性低估 eviction 的 downstream 损伤——eviction\\n    是 structured error（完整丢弃 token），量化是 unstructured error（均匀噪声），MSE 无法区分两者的级联影响。形式上\\n    MVP gate PASS 但实质不成立（在 catastrophic regime 的 false positive）。7B 实验已提交继续跑，确认 pattern\\n    是否 model-agnostic。同时需准备 metric 修正：(a) layer-output MSE 替代 attention-output MSE；(b)\\n    eviction distortion penalty；(c) gate criteria 收紧排除 PPL>2x baseline 的 regime。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: sanity_004\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: crossover exists but metric-PPL decoupled\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D001\\n  - D003\\n- id: D005\\n  date: 2026-05-13 22:03 UTC\\n  summary: MVP 实质 FAIL：attention MSE 被 falsify，pivot 到 chunk-level PPL distortion\\n    metric\\n  rationale: 7B 结果灾难性确认 metric-PPL 脱钩：budget=0.25 下 MSE 最优方案(Full MCKP) PPL=1084 vs\\n    MSE 最差方案(uniform 4-bit) PPL=15.45，反差 70×。更关键的是 quant-only MCKP (5574) vs uniform\\n    4-bit (15.45) 证明 MSE 对 quantization bit 分配同样失效——问题不仅限于 eviction。问题随模型规模指数级恶化（1.5B\\n    比 1.2×, 7B 比 70×）。被 falsify 的是 attention output MSE 作为 distortion proxy，不是 per-head\\n    MCKP framework 或 crossover 假设。立即 pivot：P0 并行跑 chunk-level PPL distortion profiling\\n    + 2-bit quant 隔离测试，验证 PPL-aligned metric 能否让 solver 做出正确分配。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: mvp_004\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: MSE-PPL 70x decoupling at 7B; MCKP allocation harmful under MSE\\n  - exp_id: sanity_004\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: MSE-PPL 1.2x decoupling at 1.5B\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D004\\n- id: D006\\n  date: 2026-05-13 23:03 UTC\\n  summary: PPL-guided MCKP 仍然失败：根因是 eviction 的 compounding effect 违反 per-head 独立性假设；转向\\n    targeted hybrid 策略\\n  rationale: PPL-based distortion profiling 结果：Full MCKP 28.07 vs Quant-only 15.45\\n    vs Uniform 15.45。换 metric 没解决问题——per-head 独立 profiling 中单 head eviction delta\\n    仅 ~0.13，但 47 heads 同时 eviction 产生 compounding effect (+18.26 PPL)。eviction 是 structured\\n    error，多头同时丢弃同一批 token，损伤不可加。Quantization 独立性成立（quant-only MCKP = uniform 15.45）。另外\\n    PPL profiling 分辨率不足（100/112 heads 显示相同 delta），需更多 eval tokens。两个可行方向：(A) targeted\\n    hybrid——仅 Layer 0 cliff heads 用 eviction（quantization 损伤极大），其余全 quantization +\\n    非均匀 bit 分配；(B) group-level eviction profiling (O(n²))。优先试 A，低成本高信息量。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: p0_1_ppl_fast\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: PPL-guided MCKP still 1.8x worse than uniform; eviction compounding effect\\n  - exp_id: p0_2_cliff_7B\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: Layer 0 is only cliff layer; 3/112 heads sensitive to 2-bit\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D005\\n- id: D007\\n  date: 2026-05-13 23:24 UTC\\n  summary: 'Pivot: 放弃 eviction arm，转向 PPL-guided per-head bit-width allocation (quant-only\\n    MCKP with {2,4,8,16}-bit)'\\n  rationale: Joint eviction+quantization 核心假设已被 falsify——eviction 的 compounding effect\\n    使 per-head 独立 profiling 原理上无法 work。但 Reviewer 指出关键盲区：之前 quant-only MCKP = uniform\\n    是 fast mode artifact（只有一个 4-bit 选项，solver 数学上只能 uniform），不是真实证据。Cliff data 证明\\n    per-head quantization sensitivity 高度不均匀（L0H0 2-bit +297% vs 大多数 head <1%）。关键实验：PPL-guided\\n    quant-only MCKP，action space {2,4,8,16}-bit，budget 4x。如果 MCKP PPL < uniform 4-bit\\n    ≥0.5 → 论文 pivot 为 rate-distortion optimal per-head bit-width allocation；如果 ≈ uniform\\n    → archive。~4h GPU 即可做 go/no-go 决策。\\n  related_experiments: []\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D006\\n- id: D008\\n  date: 2026-05-13 23:54 UTC\\n  summary: 'exp_b MIXED: 假设验证(手动24%↑)但solver失败; 采用Path B去掉2-bit修复solver'\\n  rationale: Reviewer 审查 exp_b full bit-width MCKP 结果：(1) 自动 solver 因 profiler 分辨率不足(84%\\n    heads delta=0.0000@2-bit)和 additive model 无法捕获 compounding effect，PPL 崩溃至 36.92\\n    且 under-budget 28%；(2) 手动 budget-matched 分配(L0@8bit, 8@2bit, rest@4bit) PPL=11.69\\n    vs uniform 15.45，改善 3.76 (24%)，远超 0.5 阈值——假设\\\"非均匀分配优于均匀\\\"成立；(3) 2-bit compounding\\n    effect 在 8-24 heads 间出现拐点，per-head 独立 profiling 无法发现此阈值。决定采用 Path B：去掉 2-bit 选项，用\\n    4/8/16-bit 三选项 MCKP，避免 compounding effect 同时仍能捕获 Layer 0 sensitivity 差异。实现后重跑\\n    solver 获取 clean MVP pass。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: exp_b_hires_quant\\n    role: primary\\n    result: mixed — manual pass, auto solver fail\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D\\n\\n... D001\\n- id: D004\\n  date: 2026-05-13 21:55 UTC\\n  summary: 1.5B sanity 暴露 attention MSE 与 PPL 脱钩；7B 继续跑但需准备 metric 修正方案\\n  rationale: Qwen2.5-1.5B sanity 结果显示：(1) crossover 只在 8x 极端压缩出现（89.3% heads），此时 PPL\\n    已崩溃（14000+）；(2) 在实用的 4x 压缩下 full MCKP 反而比 quant-only 差 2.77 PPL，甚至比 uniform 4-bit\\n    差 3.41 PPL；(3) 根因是 attention output MSE 系统性低估 eviction 的 downstream 损伤——eviction\\n    是 structured error（完整丢弃 token），量化是 unstructured error（均匀噪声），MSE 无法区分两者的级联影响。形式上\\n    MVP gate PASS 但实质不成立（在 catastrophic regime 的 false positive）。7B 实验已提交继续跑，确认 pattern\\n    是否 model-agnostic。同时需准备 metric 修正：(a) layer-output MSE 替代 attention-output MSE；(b)\\n    eviction distortion penalty；(c) gate criteria 收紧排除 PPL>2x baseline 的 regime。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: sanity_004\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: crossover exists but metric-PPL decoupled\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D001\\n  - D003\\n- id: D005\\n  date: 2026-05-13 22:03 UTC\\n  summary: MVP 实质 FAIL：attention MSE 被 falsify，pivot 到 chunk-level PPL distortion\\n    metric\\n  rationale: 7B 结果灾难性确认 metric-PPL 脱钩：budget=0.25 下 MSE 最优方案(Full MCKP) PPL=1084 vs\\n    MSE 最差方案(uniform 4-bit) PPL=15.45，反差 70×。更关键的是 quant-only MCKP (5574) vs uniform\\n    4-bit (15.45) 证明 MSE 对 quantization bit 分配同样失效——问题不仅限于 eviction。问题随模型规模指数级恶化（1.5B\\n    比 1.2×, 7B 比 70×）。被 falsify 的是 attention output MSE 作为 distortion proxy，不是 per-head\\n    MCKP framework 或 crossover 假设。立即 pivot：P0 并行跑 chunk-level PPL distortion profiling\\n    + 2-bit quant 隔离测试，验证 PPL-aligned metric 能否让 solver 做出正确分配。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: mvp_004\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: MSE-PPL 70x decoupling at 7B; MCKP allocation harmful under MSE\\n  - exp_id: sanity_004\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: MSE-PPL 1.2x decoupling at 1.5B\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D004\\n- id: D006\\n  date: 2026-05-13 23:03 UTC\\n  summary: PPL-guided MCKP 仍然失败：根因是 eviction 的 compounding effect 违反 per-head 独立性假设；转向\\n    targeted hybrid 策略\\n  rationale: PPL-based distortion profiling 结果：Full MCKP 28.07 vs Quant-only 15.45\\n    vs Uniform 15.45。换 metric 没解决问题——per-head 独立 profiling 中单 head eviction delta\\n    仅 ~0.13，但 47 heads 同时 eviction 产生 compounding effect (+18.26 PPL)。eviction 是 structured\\n    error，多头同时丢弃同一批 token，损伤不可加。Quantization 独立性成立（quant-only MCKP = uniform 15.45）。另外\\n    PPL profiling 分辨率不足（100/112 heads 显示相同 delta），需更多 eval tokens。两个可行方向：(A) targeted\\n    hybrid——仅 Layer 0 cliff heads 用 eviction（quantization 损伤极大），其余全 quantization +\\n    非均匀 bit 分配；(B) group-level eviction profiling (O(n²))。优先试 A，低成本高信息量。\\n  related_experiments:\\n  - exp_id: p0_1_ppl_fast\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: PPL-guided MCKP still 1.8x worse than uniform; eviction compounding effect\\n  - exp_id: p0_2_cliff_7B\\n    role: evidence\\n    result: Layer 0 is only cliff layer; 3/112 heads sensitive to 2-bit\\n  depends_on:\\n  - D005\\n- id: D007\\n  date: 2026-05-13 23:24 UTC\\n  summary: 'Pivot: 放弃 eviction arm，转向 PPL-guided per-head bit-width allocation (quant-only\\n    MCKP with {2,4,8,16}-bit)'\\n  rationale: Joint eviction+quantization 核心假设已被 falsify——eviction 的 compounding effect\\n    使 per-head 独立 profiling 原理上无法 work。但 Reviewer 指出关键盲区：之前 quant-only MCKP = uniform\\n    是 fast mode artifact（只有一个 4-bit 选项，solver 数学上只能 uniform），不是真实证据。Cliff data 证明\\n    per-head quantization sensitivity 高度不均匀（L0H0 2-bit +297% vs 大多数 head <1%）。关键实验：PPL-guided\\n    quant-only MCKP，action space {2,4,8,16}-bit，budget 4x。如果 MCKP PPL < uniform 4-bit\\n    ≥0.5 → 论文 pivot 为 rate-distortion optimal per-head bit-width allocation；如果 ≈ uniform\\n    → archive。~4h GPU 即可做 go/no-go 决策。\\n  related_experiments: []\\n  depends_on:\\n  -
